In addition, the ML rate, the exponential rate of sampling the ML string, is for the first time exploited for QAOA angle design in MIMO detection. The energy evaluator is $q$-free at $O(p\,4^p)$ cost while the ML rate transfers angles from a fixed $q_{\rm ref}$-qubit reference. Tests reach 4096-QAM, 128 antennas, $p=30$ and per-symbol SNR 0-45 dB. In simulations, ML rates fall as a power law $r_0\,p^{-α}$, with larger exponents for the sampling design, which tracks exact ML at $5\times5$ 16-QAM (0-20 dB) and $3\times3$ 64-QAM (8-28 dB) while its bit-error rate (BER) advantage widens with SNR to two orders of magnitude. The approach points toward near-optimum decoding on deeper noiseless fault-tolerant quantum (FTQ) circuits.

</details>


### [147] [Observation of magnetic quantum phase crossovers in a semiconductor spin ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17789)
*Elizaveta Morozova,Xin Zhang,Utso Bhattacharya,Pablo Cova Fariña,Daniel Jirovec,Alexander Nico-Katz,Stefan D. Oosterhout,Sougato Bose,Giordano Scappucci,Menno Veldhorst,Eugene Demler,Lieven M. K. Vandersypen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在锗量子点阵列中实现了可编程的海森堡自旋梯，通过连续可调的交换相互作用，在固定磁场下绘制了梯级单态、倾斜反铁磁和完全极化三个相图。结合平衡态与动力学测量的哈密顿量学习协议定量刻画了梯子，包含自旋轨道相互作用以复现交叉行为，并通过非传统体探针可及的高阶自旋关联函数（包括四点关联）揭示了有限尺寸下的相结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联量子磁体的集体相依赖于具有精确微观控制的理论可处理模型系统。反铁磁自旋梯为半填充时的门控相与无能隙相以及掺杂时的非常规配对倾向提供了理想平台，但此前缺乏可编程、连续可调且能进行微观级表征的实验平台。

Method: 在半填充锗量子点阵列中构建可编程海森堡自旋梯，实现位点分辨且连续可调的交换相互作用。在固定磁场下改变梯级（rung）与腿（leg）耦合以映射相图；采用结合平衡态与动力学测量的哈密顿量学习协议定量表征，纳入自旋轨道相互作用；测量包含四点相关函数在内的高阶自旋关联器以探测相结构。

Result: 成功绘制了梯级单态、倾斜反铁磁和完全极化三个相的相图；哈密顿量学习协议结合自旋轨道相互作用定量复现了观测到的交叉行为；高阶自旋关联函数（含四点相关）揭示了有限尺寸下潜在的相结构信号。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了锗量子点阵列作为量子磁性可控平台的可行性，为研究掺杂梯中的非常规超导性开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding collective phases of strongly correlated quantum magnets relies on theoretically tractable model systems with precise microscopic control. Antiferromagnetic spin ladders provide such a setting, hosting field-tunable gapped and gapless phases at half filling and unconventional pairing tendencies upon doping. Here, we realize a programmable Heisenberg spin ladder in a half-filled germanium quantum dot array featuring site-resolved, continuously tunable exchange interactions. Under a fixed magnetic field, we vary the rung and leg coupling to map the rung-singlet, canted antiferromagnetic, and fully polarized phases. Hamiltonian-learning protocols combining equilibrium and dynamical measurements quantitatively characterize the ladder, incorporating spin-orbit interactions to reproduce the observed crossover behavior. Measurements of higher-order spin correlators -- including four-point correlations inaccessible to conventional bulk probes -- reveal signatures of the underlying phase structure despite the finite size. Our results establish germanium quantum dot arrays as a controllable platform for quantum magnetism, opening routes to investigate unconventional superconductivity in doped ladders.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamics of Majorana tetron qubits under quasiparticle poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18042)
*Sauri Bhattacharyya,Bernard van Heck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Majorana四端子（tetron）量子比特在外在准粒子中毒（通过外部导线耦合导致）下的耗散动力学。从描述含四个Majorana零模的有限尺寸拓扑超导体的Bloch-Redfield方程出发，推导出稳态、宇称泄漏率和退相干率的解析表达式，适用于任意充电能值。分析表明，退相速率的指数抑制会因量子比特态的能量劈裂而逐渐被移除，这些结果对理解Majorana量子比特原型中的时域实验数据具有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana量子比特是拓扑量子计算的重要候选方案，其在实际器件中不可避免地会受到外在准粒子中毒效应的影响，导致退相干和宇称泄漏。需要定量理解这种耗散机制在不同参数（尤其是充电能）下的行为，以指导实验设计和数据解释。

Method: 采用Bloch-Redfield方程对含四个Majorana零模的有限尺寸拓扑超导体进行建模，从该主方程解析推导出稳态性质、宇称泄漏率以及退相干率在任意充电能值下的表达式。

Result: 获得了稳态、宇称泄漏率和退相干率的解析表达式，并证明量子比特态间的能量劈裂会逐渐消除退相速率的指数抑制效应。

Conclusion: 该理论结果可用于理解和分析Majorana量子比特原型器件的时域实验数据，揭示了外在准粒子中毒对退相干影响的物理机制。

Abstract: We study the dissipative dynamics of a Majorana tetron qubit in the presence of extrinsic quasiparticle poisoning due to the coupling to external leads. From the Bloch-Redfield equation describing a finite-size topological superconductor hosting four Majorana zero modes, we recover analytical expressions for the steady state, the parity leakage rate, and the decoherence rate of a Majorana qubit at arbitrary values of the charging energy. The analysis shows that the exponential suppression of the dephasing rate is gradually removed by the energy splitting of the qubit states. These results can be useful to understand time-domain experimental data in Majorana qubit prototypes.

</details>


### [149] [Long-time fermionic quantum transport with controlled full-state error using an adaptive reservoir-mode window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18049)
*Mikhail Umanskii,Nataliya Arefyeva,Georgy Sultanov,Alexey Rubtsov,Evgeny Polyakov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为"磁带录音机粗粒化"(tape-recorder coarse graining)的方法，用于模拟与费米子库耦合的相互作用纳米结构的实时动力学。该方法将每个无相互作用导带重组为入射模式、活动模式和出射模式，仅用活动模式演化系统，并对出射模式进行随机采样和截断。作者推导了截断误差的非微扰上界，并通过多种基准测试验证了方法的准确性、效率和长期模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时模拟与费米子库耦合的相互作用纳米结构时，随着时间推移需要越来越多的环境自由度来保留长时关联，这导致计算成本急剧增长。现有方法（如有限指数HEOM分解）在代数衰减关联的平带库情形下无法收敛，因此需要一种能够高效处理长时间演化的新方法。

Method: 该方法将每个无相互作用的库重组为三类模式：入射模式（incoming）、活动模式（active）和出射模式（outgoing）。系统仅用活动模式进行传播，出射模式在其剩余积分耦合低于给定阈值时被随机采样并移除。作者推导了每次出射模式截断导致的截断态与精确态之间保真度损失的非微扰上界，该上界依赖于模式的剩余耦合权重和有限区间响应因子。在数值实现上，活动模式数在固定相对阈值下随时间饱和，并随阈值减小呈对数增长。

Result: 在双位点量子点接触（零温度、最大偏压）上进行了基准测试。对于洛伦兹库，动力学与收敛的HEOM计算一致，稳态电流符合Landauer-Büttiker结果。对于具有代数衰减关联的平带库，该方法与直接薛定谔演化在有限尺寸回波之前一致，并复现Landauer-Büttiker稳态电流，而有限指数HEOM分解无法收敛。对于相互作用接触，该方法再现了库仑阻塞峰劈裂。在无相互作用驱动极限下，与精确Floquet格林函数计算一致，并复现周期驱动下的相干电流抑制，该抑制在有限库仑排斥下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 磁带录音机粗粒化方法使得对相互作用费米子输运中完整的器件-库态进行实用的长时间模拟成为可能。该方法在多种基准测试中均展示了准确性，包括长时动力学、稳态电流、相互作用效应以及周期驱动情形，为量子输运模拟提供了一种新的高效工具。

Abstract: Real-time simulations of interacting nanostructures coupled to fermionic reservoirs can require a growing number of environmental degrees of freedom to retain long-lived correlations. We introduce tape-recorder coarse graining, which reorganizes each noninteracting lead into incoming, active, and outgoing modes. The device is propagated with the active modes, while outgoing modes are stochastically sampled and removed once their remaining integrated coupling falls below a prescribed threshold. For each outgoing-mode truncation, we derive a nonperturbative upper bound on the infidelity between the exact and truncated full device--reservoir states over any prescribed finite interval. The bound depends on the mode's remaining coupling weight and finite-interval response factors. Numerically, the active-mode count saturates in time at fixed relative threshold and grows logarithmically as the threshold is reduced. We benchmark the method on a two-site quantum point contact at zero temperature and maximal bias. For Lorentzian reservoirs, the dynamics agrees with converged HEOM calculations and the steady-state current with the Landauer--Büttiker result. For flat-band reservoirs with algebraically decaying correlations, it agrees with direct Schrödinger evolution before finite-size recurrences and reproduces the Landauer--Büttiker stationary current, while finite exponential HEOM decompositions remain unconverged. For interacting contacts, the method yields Coulomb-blockade peak splitting. In the noninteracting driven limit, it agrees with an exact Floquet Green-function calculation and reproduces coherent current suppression under periodic driving, which persists at finite Coulomb repulsion. Together, these benchmarks show that tape-recorder coarse graining enables practical long-time simulations of the full device--reservoir state in interacting fermionic transport.

</details>


### [150] [Ultrafast and high resolution spatial light modulation for cold atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18071)
*Alexander Dennisovich Deters,Yanfei Li,Alexander Douglas,Markus Greiner,Aaron W. Young*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于超冷原子量子计算与模拟的先进光学系统，实现了任意图案生成、极高帧率（>84 MFPS）和高空间分辨率，并展示了其在可编程Hubbard模型模拟和快速二维光镊排列操作方面的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超冷原子量子平台中，所有局部控制都通过光学场实现，因此管理多体量子态本质上成为光学设计问题。现有系统在速度、分辨率、低无序度等方面存在折衷，亟需一种在规模相当的同时全面优化其他性能指标的光学系统。

Method: 设计并构建了一种光学系统，能够在保持与已有方法相当规模的同时，实现10⁻³强度分辨率、超过84 MFPS的帧率，以及83×52光束腰的空间分辨率（其中11×52可通过单个40 GHz电光调制器访问）。在此基础上，开发并数值验证了完全可编程Hubbard模型的实现方案，支持对局域化学势、隧穿幅度、在位相互作用和人工磁通图案的时变控制；同时该系统支持二维光镊的快速任意排列操作。

Result: 系统实现了任意图案生成、10⁻³强度分辨率、>84 MFPS帧率和83×52光束腰的空间分辨率。数值验证了完全可编程Hubbard模型方案，并展示了该架构可执行快速任意二维光镊排列操作。

Conclusion: 该光学系统在保持规模优势的同时全面提升了速度、分辨率和灵活性，解锁了新一代实验类型，包括完全可编程的Hubbard模型模拟，以及将光学约束与高速纠错码设计解耦的可能性。

Abstract: Programmable arrays of ultracold atoms are a leading platform for quantum computation and simulation, enabling state-of-the-art implementations of quantum error correction, and analog simulations of Hubbard models that address open problems in condensed matter physics. In these systems, all local control is mediated through precisely shaped optical fields, and so the challenge of managing many-body quantum states becomes an exercise in optical design. In particular, one wishes for fast, flexible control with low disorder and heating, and access to large arrays with many atoms. An ideal optical system therefore must generate arbitrary patterns with high spatial resolution and low disorder, and alter these patterns on a timescale that is faster than the relevant atomic dynamics. Here, we present an optical system that is comparable to previous approaches in scale, while advancing all other axes. We demonstrate arbitrary pattern generation with $10^{-3}$ intensity resolution, a frame rate of $>84$ MFPS (megaframes per second), and a spatial resolution of $83 \times 52$ beam waists (with $11 \times 52$ waists accessible via a single $40$ GHz electro-optic modulator). These capabilities unlock a new class of experiments. We develop and numerically validate a scheme for fully programmable Hubbard models, with time-dependent control over local chemical potentials, tunneling amplitudes, on-site interactions, and patterns of artificial magnetic flux. The same architecture performs fast, arbitrary permutations of tweezers in 2D, decoupling optical constraints from the design of high-rate error-correcting codes.

</details>
